仓库 None

─── [1] 用户 ───
查看仓库，为我解释该工具选取模型的逻辑，然后以尽量小的改动添加CrispASR自由选取模型的功能。
GUI中，原版功能存在于模型-推理引擎-CrispASR-选择模型，其服从预设的列表。
两种方案（各1个分支，便于PR。1可能是2的基础，建议按顺序完成）：
1. 最小化，只添加改变主模型的选项“从本地选择……”（FA永远使用预设）
2. 在下方添加新的“选择FA模型”，默认值便是原预设，除此之外就是“从本地选择……”，原来的选择模型同步改为“选择主模型”
最后，再写一下PR文案。
资料：
CrispASR官方文档说：Qwen3-ForcedAligner 可以挂载到任何转录后端上，包括 Whisper。

   1 | """
   2 | crisp_engine.py — CrispASR (ggml / Vulkan) Whisper 推理后端
   3 | 
   4 | 定位：把 CrispASR 的 whisper backend 当作本项目的第三个推理引擎（与
   5 | ASREngine[OpenVINO] 并列），用来加载 Whisper
   6 | 类模型（默认 OpenAI Whisper 官方 GGML）。
   7 | 
   8 | 设计重点：
   9 |   • CrispASR 是 whisper.cpp fork，crispasr.exe 自己会输出 SRT（含分段时间轴），
  10 |     因此本引擎不需重造 VAD/chunk/FA，本质是「调用 crispasr.exe → 读 SRT →
  11 |     写回」。
  12 |   • GPU 后端由 load(gpu_backend=) 决定，对应「使用者选的 CrispASR 加速版本」
  13 |     （downloader.crispasr_gpu_backend）：
  14 |       - vulkan（默认）：Windows 通吃 Intel/AMD 核显与 NVIDIA 独显，体积最小。
  15 |       - cuda：NVIDIA 专用，需下载 CUDA build（0.8.32 起在 Blackwell 实测正常；
  16 |         早期 0.7.1 的 fattn crash 为 nemotron 后端专属问题）。
  17 |       - None：CPU build，改挂 -ng（该 build 没有 ggml-vulkan/cuda DLL）。
  18 |   • 界面对齐 app.py 既有引擎契约：load / ready / transcribe / process_file /
  19 |     processor / diar_engine / use_aligner / aligner / _lock。
  20 | 
  21 | 对外界面（app.py 的 _load_models[crispasr 分支] 会这样调用）：
  22 |     eng = CrispWhisperEngine()
  23 |     eng.load(model_path=..., crispasr_dir=..., device_id=0, cb=set_status)
  24 |     srt = eng.process_file(audio_path, progress_cb=..., language=..., ...)
  25 | """

  1 | """fa_aligner.py — chatllm 原生 ForcedAligner（后端无关，无需 PyTorch）

  9 |   • CPU 用 `-ngl 0`，GPU 用 `-ngl {device_id}:all`。

 92 | def _find_aligner_gguf(*dirs: Path) -> Path | None:
 93 |     """依序在给定目录查找 qwen3 ForcedAligner GGUF（取第一个符合者）。
 94 | 
 95 |     调用端给的顺序是「模型目录 → crispasr 目录」：新版下载到模型目录，
 96 |     旧版留在核心目录的档仍能找到，不必重新下载。
 97 |     """
 98 |     seen: set[str] = set()
 99 |     for d in dirs:
100 |         if not d:
101 |             continue
102 |         d = Path(d)
103 |         try:
104 |             key = str(d.resolve())
105 |         except OSError:
106 |             key = str(d)
107 |         if key in seen:
108 |             continue
109 |         seen.add(key)
110 |         for g in sorted(d.glob(_ALIGNER_GLOB)):
111 |             if g.is_file():
112 |                 return g
113 |     return None
114 | 
115 | 
116 | def _infer_backend(model_path: Path | str) -> str:
117 |     """依模型文件名推断 crispasr 后端（--backend）。
118 | 
119 |     CrispASR 是多后端 runtime（whisper / qwen3 / parakeet …），同一支
120 |     crispasr.exe 靠 `--backend` 切换模型架构。OpenAI Whisper GGML 属 whisper
121 |     架构（默认，免标志）；Qwen3-ASR GGUF 文件名含 'qwen3' → 走 qwen3 后端，1.7B
122 |     用 'qwen3-1.7b'。判断错误时 crispasr 会自报模型不符，不致默默产生垃圾。
123 |     """
124 |     name = Path(model_path).name.lower()
125 |     if "qwen3" in name:
126 |         return "qwen3-1.7b" if "1.7b" in name else "qwen3"
127 |     return "whisper"

〔思考〕
Now I understand fully. Let me summarize the model selection logic:

1. GUI (index.html + modelView.js): `getModelOptions()` → cores from `_MODEL_CATALOG` (model_catalog.py). Selecting a model → `setModel(core, model)` → writes settings: `backend`, `crisp_model`, `whisper_size` / `crisp_qwen_quant`.
2. Backend `_load_crispasr()`: reads settings, decides model_path: qwen3 GGUF in model_dir via `_qwen_casr_dl`, or whisper GGML in crispasr_dir; downloads if missing. Then finds aligner GGUF (`find_aligner_gguf(model_dir, fa_quant, crispasr_dir)`) with pattern `qwen3-forced-aligner-0.6b-*.gguf`, downloads if missing.
3. `CrispWhisperEngine.load()`: backend inferred from filename (`_infer_backend`: "qwen3" in name → qwen3-1.7b/qwen3; else whisper). FA detected, `_fa_incompatible` check, then `_build_cmd` adds `-am <gguf> -falign`.
4. FA docs: Qwen3-ForcedAligner can be attached to any transcription backend, including Whisper.

Now the task: add locally-chosen model feature:
Branch 1 (minimal): add "从本地选择……" option for the main model (FA always preset).
Branch 2: add a new "选择FA模型" selector default = preset, plus "从本地选择……", and rename original to "选择主模型".

**Design (Branch 1 — 只加"从本地选择……" for main model):**

Settings key: `crisp_local_model` (string path). When set and a CrispASR model is chosen:
- `_load_crispasr`: if `crisp_local_model` is non-empty path and exists → use it as model_path, skip the download logic (and skip qwen/whisper specific checks). Backend inferred from filename by crisp_engine.
- `get_model_options` / catalog: add sentinel entry `本地模型…` in CrispASR core models list, plus the current externally chosen label. Frontend: when selecting that option, call `API.browseFile()` to pick a file, then a new API `setLocalModel(path, role)` which persists `crisp_local_model` and returns description. Then the dropdown display shows the local file name.
- `_current_selection`: if `crisp_local_model` set → return (CrispASR, "本地：" + filename) but need to keep it selectable in dropdown. Simplest: catalog label is fixed "从本地选择…", and display of chosen file can come separately. Hmm, but the dropdown's selected option must exist in list. So: add a dynamic entry to the CrispASR core models list when `crisp_local_model` is set: label = `本地模型：<basename>`; and always append the action option `从本地选择…`. - health_check: not critical, leave.
- `set_model`: needs to handle the sentinel label — when entry not found, it falls back to first CrispASR entry, which would be wrong. Need to guard: if model_label is the local sentinel, don't reset crisp_model. Better: handle `setLocalModel` separately via new API method, not via set_model. ```python
# ── 本地自选模型（CrispASR）─────────────────────────────────────────────
# 预设目录之外的 .bin/.gguf 也能用：settings 记绝对路径，加载时优先于预设。
#   crisp_local_model → 主模型（Whisper GGML / Qwen3-ASR GGUF，后端依文件名推断）
#   crisp_local_fa    → 时间轴对齐器（ForcedAligner GGUF；Qwen3-ForcedAligner 可挂
#                       任何转录后端，含 Whisper）
# 下拉里的两项特殊条目：已选的「本地模型：<文件名>」与动作项「从本地选择…」。
_LOCAL_PICK = "从本地选择…"
_LOCAL_PREFIX = "本地模型："
_LOCAL_MIN_BYTES = 1 << 20          # 1 MB 下限：拦下误选的空档/LFS pointer


def _local_model(s: dict, key: str) -> Path | None:
    """settings[key] 指向的本地模型档（存在且看起来完整才返回，否则 None）。"""
    raw = str(s.get(key) or "").strip()
    if not raw:
        return None
    p = Path(raw).expanduser()
    try:
        return p if p.is_file() and p.stat().st_size >= _LOCAL_MIN_BYTES else None
    except OSError:
        return None
```

Now `set_local_model`:

```python
    def set_local_model(self, path: str, kind: str = "model") -> dict:
        """把使用者自选的模型档记进 settings（CrispASR 专用）。

        kind="model" → 主模型（crisp_local_model；Whisper GGML / Qwen3-ASR GGUF）
        kind="fa"    → 时间轴对齐器（crisp_local_fa；ForcedAligner GGUF，
                       Qwen3-ForcedAligner 可挂在任何转录后端上，含 Whisper）
        传空路径 = 清除该自选项，回到预设。
        """
        key = {"fa": "crisp_local_fa"}.get(kind, "crisp_local_model")
        raw = str(path or "").strip().strip('"')
        if not raw:
            self.settings.update({key: ""})
            return {"ok": True, "cleared": True,
                    "message": "已改回预设模型。"}
        p = Path(raw).expanduser()
        if not p.is_file():
            return {"ok": False, "error": f"找不到文件：{p}"}
        if p.suffix.lower() not in (".bin", ".gguf"):
            return {"ok": False, "error": "请选择 .bin（Whisper GGML）或 .gguf（Qwen3-ASR / 对齐器）模型档。"}
        if p.stat().st_size < _LOCAL_MIN_BYTES:
            return {"ok": False, "error": "该文件过小，不像是完整模型。"}
        self.settings.update({key: str(p)})
        return {"ok": True, "path": str(p), "name": p.name,
                "message": f"已选定本地{'对齐' if key.endswith('_fa') else ''}模型「{p.name}」，点「下载并加载模型」即可载入。"}
```

Note that `settings.update` doesn't change backend; if backend is openvino and user picks local model, they'd need to switch core. Frontend only shows the picker under CrispASR core? Actually the dropdown for OpenVINO core doesn't include local entries, so fine. But if user selects local while core=crispasr, backend stays crispasr. Good.

Should `set_local_model` also set `backend: "crispasr"`? The picker is only visible for CrispASR core, and core switch calls set_model first. Keep it simple but safe: include `"backend": "crispasr"` for kind=model? Hmm, if the user is on OpenVINO and somehow calls it... Let's set backend crispasr for kind="model" only when the persisted backend is crispasr? Simpler: don't touch backend. GUI guarantees.

`browse_file` kind param:
```python
    def browse_file(self, start: str = "", title: str = "选择文件",
                    kind: str = "exe") -> dict:
        ...
        types = {
            "model": [("Whisper / Qwen3 模型（*.bin *.gguf）", "*.bin *.gguf"), ("所有文件", "*.*")],
            "fa": [("ForcedAligner（*.gguf）", "*.gguf"), ("所有文件", "*.*")],
        }.get(kind) or [("可执行文件", "*.exe"), ("所有文件", "*.*")]
```
Note tkinter filetypes patterns can be "*.bin *.gguf" in one string? tkinter passes to Tk; multiple patterns in one string separated by spaces works on Windows ("*.bin *.gguf")? Safer: [("模型文件", "*.bin;*.gguf")] on Windows uses semicolon. Cross-platform: Tk accepts a space-separated list in the pattern string. Actually Tk's filetypes pattern is a list like {"*.txt"} and supports multiple patterns: pattern can be a Tcl list. In Python, passing "*.bin *.gguf" gets Tcl-split into a list of two patterns. Yes, that works.

```js
    async browseFile(start, title, kind) {
      if (MODE === "web") { try { return await apiPost("/api/browse-file", { start, title, kind }, { timeoutMs: 0 }); } catch {} }
      return { ok: false, path: "" };
    },
    async setLocalModel(path, kind) {
      if (MODE === "web") return apiPost("/api/set-local-model", { path, kind });
      return { ok: false, error: "mock 模式不支持本地模型" };
    },
```

- `populateModels`: mark action options; and a separate handling.
```js
function populateModels(coreLabel, modelLabel) {
  const core = coreObj(coreLabel); if (!core) return;
  const sel = $("#model-select"); sel.innerHTML = "";
  core.models.forEach(m => {
    const o = document.createElement("option");
    o.value = m.label; o.textContent = m.label; o.dataset.arch = m.arch;
    o.dataset.note = m.note || "";
    if (m.action) o.dataset.action = m.action;
    if (m.local) o.dataset.local = "1";
    if (m.label === modelLabel) o.selected = true;
    sel.appendChild(o);
  });
  ...
}
```
`updateArch()` uses selectedOptions[0].dataset.arch etc. For local entries arch="CrispASR · 本地文件", fine.

And:
```js
async function pickLocalModel(kind) {
  let r;
  try { r = await API.browseFile("", kind === "fa" ? "选择时间轴对齐模型（GGUF）" : "选择模型文件（.bin / .gguf）", kind); }
  catch (err) { ... }
  if (!r || !r.ok || !r.path) { await renderModel(); return; }   // 取消 → 回弹当前选择
  const res = await API.setLocalModel(r.path, kind);
  if (!res || res.ok === false) { modelMsg((res && res.error) || "套用失败", "warn"); await renderModel(); return; }
  modelMsg(res.message, "info");
  await renderModel();     // 重建下拉：出现「本地模型：xxx」并被选中
  updateLoadBtn();
}
```

```python
def _local_file_ok(p) -> bool:
    try:
        p = Path(p)
        return p.is_file() and p.stat().st_size >= _LOCAL_MIN_BYTES
    except OSError:
        return False
```

```js
function populateModels(coreLabel, modelLabel) {
  const core = coreObj(coreLabel); if (!core) return;
  const sel = $("#model-select"); sel.innerHTML = "";
  core.models.forEach(m => {
    const o = document.createElement("option");
    o.value = m.label; o.textContent = m.label; o.dataset.arch = m.arch;
    o.dataset.note = m.note || "";
    if (m.label === modelLabel) o.selected = true;
    sel.appendChild(o);
  });
  if (!core.models.some(m => m.label === modelLabel) && core.models[0]) sel.value = core.models[0].label;
  updateArch();
}
```

```js
function populateModels(coreLabel, modelLabel) {
  const core = coreObj(coreLabel); if (!core) return;
  const sel = $("#model-select"); sel.innerHTML = "";
  core.models.forEach(m => {
    const o = document.createElement("option");
    o.value = m.label; o.textContent = m.label; o.dataset.arch = m.arch;
    o.dataset.note = m.note || "";
    if (m.action) o.dataset.action = m.action;   // "browse" = 动作项，不是真模型
    if (m.label === modelLabel) o.selected = true;
    sel.appendChild(o);
  });
  if (!core.models.some(m => m.label === modelLabel) && core.models[0]) sel.value = core.models[0].label;
  updateArch();
}
```

```js
// 换模型 → 套用；选中「从本地选择…」→ 开原生文件对话框
$("#model-select").addEventListener("change", async e => {
  const opt = e.target.selectedOptions[0];
  if (opt && opt.dataset.action === "browse") { await pickLocalModel("model"); return; }
  updateArch();
  await applyModel(_curCore, e.target.value);
});

// 本地自选模型（kind: "model" 主模型 / "fa" 对齐器）：选文件 → 写 settings → 刷新下拉
async function pickLocalModel(kind) {
  const title = kind === "fa" ? T("model.pickFaTitle", "选择时间轴对齐模型（*.gguf）")
                              : T("model.pickModelTitle", "选择模型文件（*.bin / *.gguf）");
  let r = null;
  try { r = await API.browseFile("", title, kind); }
  catch (err) { modelMsg(String(err), "warn"); }
  if (!r || !r.ok || !r.path) { await refreshModelOptions(null); return; }   // 取消 → 回弹
  let res = null;
  try { res = await API.setLocalModel(r.path, kind); }
  catch (err) { modelMsg(T("model.applyFail", "套用失败：") + (err.message || err), "warn"); }
  await refreshModelOptions(res && res.message);
  if (res && res.error) modelMsg(res.error, "warn");
}

// 重新拉取目录与当前选择并重建下拉（不重画设备/自检，避免闪动）
async function refreshModelOptions(message) {
  try {
    _modelOpt = await API.getModelOptions();
    _curCore = _modelOpt.current.core;
    renderCoreCards();
    populateModels(_curCore, _modelOpt.current.model);
    if (message) modelMsg(message, "info");
    updateLoadBtn();
  } catch (e) {}
}

先把「模型是怎么被选出来的」这条链路说清楚，再动手改。我已经把相关代码都读完了。

## 现在的选区逻辑（4 段链路）

**① 目录（唯一的“真理源”）** — `model_catalog.py` 的 `_MODEL_CATALOG`，每行是 `(引擎标签, 模型标签, backend, settings patch)`。GUI 的下拉列表就是它；选一项 = 把 patch 写进 `settings.json`（`crisp_model` + `whisper_size`／`crisp_qwen_quant`）。

**② 点选** — `webview_backend.set_model()` 按标签反查目录项 → `settings.update({"backend": ..., **patch})`。之后 `_current_selection()` 把 settings *反推*成标签，用来回填下拉；`_identity_for()` 把 settings 压成指纹，决定“要不要重新加载”。

**③ 加载** — `_load_crispasr()` 读 settings 决定 `model_path`：Qwen3 系列 → `model_dir/下载器给的文件名`；否则 → `crispasr_dir/ggml-*.bin`；缺档就自动下载。FA 对齐器是**独立**的一支：`find_aligner_gguf()` 按固定文件名 `qwen3-forced-aligner-0.6b-<quant>.gguf` 找（模型目录优先、crispasr 目录备援），缺则下载。

**④ 引擎** — `CrispWhisperEngine.load()` 不看 settings，只看**文件名**：`_infer_backend()` 里 `"qwen3" in name → qwen3-1.7b`，否则 `whisper`。然后 `_build_cmd()` 拼 `-m 模型`、`-am 对齐器 -falign`。

关键洞察：**主模型和 FA 是两条互不依赖的支路**，而且模型路径只由“文件名”参与推断——这正是能塞进“本地自选”的位置。

## 方案1 的最小改动设计

只加一个 settings 键 `crisp_local_model`（绝对路径），并让它**优先于预设**；FA 完全不动。下拉里追加两项：已选中的 `本地模型：<文件名>`（动态项）和动作项 `从本地选择…`（点它开原生文件对话框，选完回调写入 settings）。共 5 个文件、无新依赖。

+_LOCAL_PREFIX = "本地模型："
+_LOCAL_MIN_BYTES = 1 << 20      # 1 MB 下限：拦下空档 / Git-LFS pointer
+
+
+def _local_file_ok(p) -> bool:
+    """路径指向的档存在且大小像完整模型（排除空档与 LFS pointer）。"""
+    try:
+        p = Path(p).expanduser()
+        return p.is_file() and p.stat().st_size >= _LOCAL_MIN_BYTES
+    except (OSError, TypeError, ValueError):
+        return False
+
+
+def _local_model(s: dict, key: str = "crisp_local_model") -> Path | None:
+    """settings[key] 指向的本地模型档；未设／失效一律 None（调用端回退预设）。"""
+    raw = str((s or {}).get(key) or "").strip()
+    if not raw:
+        return None
+    p = Path(raw).expanduser()
+    return p if _local_file_ok(p) else None

- Files I still need to edit: webview_server.py (CRLF!), i18n.js (CRLF!), bridge.js (LF), modelView.js (LF), index.html (LF).

So when I edit webview_server.py and i18n.js I must preserve CRLF, otherwise the whole file shows as rewritten in the diff... Actually no: if I insert lines with LF into a CRLF file, git diff will show only those lines changed but with a "mixed line endings" artifact — the diff could include the whole line ending mismatch? Git diff compares line content including CR. Inserting LF-only lines into a CRLF file: only the added lines differ (their content lacks CR), so the diff shows only the added lines — plus possibly `^M` noise. It doesn't rewrite other lines. But the file becomes mixed EOL, which is bad practice. Better: preserve CRLF in those files. 

My editor writes `new_text` as given — with `\n`. So new lines would be LF. Hmm. Can I include `\r` in new_text? I can type `\r` literally? The JSON string could contain "\r\n" escapes. The tool takes new_text as a string; if I pass actual CR characters, they'd be written. In the JSON I can write "\r\n" escape sequences which become real CR LF. But the exact old_text match also needs CRLF — matching is probably exact on the file content, so old_text must include \r. I can't reliably type CR in the JSON... Actually I can: in JSON I can use "\r\n" escape. The tool receives real CRLF chars. That should work for both old_text and new_text as long as the file uses CRLF everywhere.

That's finicky and error-prone. Alternative approach for CRLF files: keep the diff minimal by making edits only in LF files, and for the CRLF files (webview_server.py route addition, i18n.js keys) use a script (python) to do the insertion preserving CRLF. Or use the editor with \r\n escapes.

Now, plan: for CRLF files, I'll do edits via a small Python one-liner that reads bytes, replaces, writes bytes — preserving CRLF. That's a shell command requiring approval, but precise.

Actually simpler: use `editor` with old_text/new_text where I embed \r? Risky to match. I'll use Python with explicit `\r\n` in the patterns — deterministic.

```python
    def browse_file(self, start: str = "", title: str = "选择文件") -> dict:
        """弹出原生「选择文件」对话框（用于 FFmpeg 可执行文件路径）。

        返回 {"ok": bool, "path": str}；用户取消 → ok=False。
        """
        try:
            import tkinter as tk
            from tkinter import filedialog
            root = tk.Tk()
            root.withdraw()
            root.attributes("-topmost", True)
            try:
                initial = Path(start) if start and Path(start).exists() else None
                path = filedialog.askopenfilename(
                    title=title, initialdir=str(initial.parent) if initial else "",
                    initialfile=initial.name if initial else "",
                    filetypes=[("可执行文件", "*.exe"), ("所有文件", "*.*")],
                    parent=root)
            finally:
                root.destroy()
            return {"ok": bool(path), "path": path or ""}
        except Exception as e:
            return {"ok": False, "path": "", "error": str(e)}
```

Answer:

- `_local_model()` 返回的是“这个路径现在还能不能用”，校验 `is_file()` + ≥1MB；文件消失 → None → `is not None` 为 False。
- 在 `_has_any_model` 里，这个 False **不会**直接 `return False`，而是继续往下查其它模型来源（预设 whisper/qwen/OV）。所以“本地模型消失”只意味着这一项不算数，不会误报 True，也不会因此把“已有模型”判成 False（如果机器上还有别的模型）。
- 另一处我有意区分：`_current_selection()` / `selected_model_present()` 用的是**原始 settings 字符串**（raw），所以文件被删后下拉仍显示「本地模型：x」，而 `selected_model_present()` 返回 False → 前端显示“未下载 + 检查明细里是那条绝对路径”。这是“意图 vs 可用性”的两层：raw 表示用户选了它，`_local_model` 表示它可用。

Then run a compile check + diff review.

修好了。同时回答你的问题：**不会 return True**。

关键在 `_local_model()` 的语义——它不是"读设置"，而是"这个档现在还能不能用"：

```python
def _local_file_ok(p) -> bool:
    return p.is_file() and p.stat().st_size >= _LOCAL_MIN_BYTES   # 消失/空档 → False
```

所以本地档被删后 `_local_model(...)` 回 `None`，`is not None` 为 `False`，`_has_any_model` 只是**跳过这一项继续往下查**（预设 whisper / qwen / OV）。它不会因为本地档没了就误报"已有模型"，但也不会因此把整机判成"没有模型"——如果 `ov_models` 里还有预设档，那仍然该是 True。

这里我刻意做了「意图 vs 可用性」两层，别混用：

| 场景 | 用原始 settings 字符串 | 用 `_local_model()` |
|---|---|---|
| `_current_selection` 下拉回显 | ✅ 文件删了仍显示「本地模型：x」 | ❌ 会悄悄跳回预设，用户看不出自己的选择没了 |
| `selected_model_present` | — | ✅ 回 False → 前端走"未下载"+检查明细 |
| `_has_any_model` | — | ✅ 只有真能加载才算"已有模型" |

也就是说：文件消失时界面会**停在**「本地模型：x 未就绪」并给出绝对路径，而不是静默换成别的模型去跑。

再自查一遍改动，顺便编译一次：

─── [112] 返回 run_commands ｜ git --no-pager diff -- webview_backend.py | grep -E '^[-+]' | grep -vE '^(\+\+\+|---)' | head -100 ───
+# ── 本地自选模型（CrispASR）──────────────────────────────────────────────
+# 预设目录之外的 .bin/.gguf 也能用：settings 记绝对路径，加载时优先于预设。
+#   crisp_local_model → 主模型（Whisper GGML / Qwen3-ASR GGUF；后端由
+#                       crisp_engine._infer_backend 依文件名推断）
+#   crisp_local_fa    → 时间轴对齐器（ForcedAligner GGUF）。官方文档：
+#                       Qwen3-ForcedAligner 可挂在任何转录后端上，含 Whisper。
+# 下拉里的两个特殊条目：已选中的「本地模型：<文件名>」与动作项「从本地选择…」。
+_LOCAL_PICK = "从本地选择…"
+_LOCAL_PREFIX = "本地模型："
+_LOCAL_MIN_BYTES = 1 << 20      # 1 MB 下限：拦下空档 / Git-LFS pointer
+
+
+def _local_file_ok(p) -> bool:
+    """路径指向的档存在且大小像完整模型（排除空档与 LFS pointer）。"""
+    try:
+        p = Path(p).expanduser()

I'll write a Python script using binary read/write to preserve CRLF. Use a heredoc in bash. The file is CRLF, so my search strings need `\r\n`.

Let me write a script:

```bash
python - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
p = Path("webview_server.py")
b = p.read_bytes()
old = (b'                    if path == "/api/model":\r\n'
       b'                        body = self._read_json_body()\r\n'
       b'                        return self._json(server.backend.set_model(\r\n'
       b'                            body.get("core"), body.get("model")))\r\n')
new = old + (b'                    if path == "/api/set-local-model":   # 本地自选模型（主模型／对齐器）\r\n'
             b'                        body = self._read_json_body()\r\n'
             b'                        return self._json(server.backend.set_local_model(\r\n'
             b'                            body.get("path"), body.get("kind", "model")))\r\n')
assert b.count(old) == 1, b.count(old)
b = b.replace(old, new)

old2 = (b'                        return self._json(server.backend.browse_file(\r\n'
        b'                            body.get("start", ""), body.get("title", "选择文件")))\r\n')
new2 = (b'                        return self._json(server.backend.browse_file(\r\n'
        b'                            body.get("start", ""), body.get("title", "选择文件"),\r\n'
        b'                            body.get("kind", "exe")))\r\n')
assert b.count(old2) == 1, b.count(old2)
b = b.replace(old2, new2)
p.write_bytes(b)
print("ok")
PY
```

Note: file has CRLF? git ls-files said i/crlf w/crlf → yes. But is there a BOM? Possibly. Doesn't matter.

〔思考〕
The user asks: since there's a separate local model mechanism, why did I change browseFile?

Answer: because the new "从本地选择…" flow needs the native file dialog, and the existing `browse_file` backend had a hard-coded filter `[("可执行文件","*.exe")]` — designed for picking ffmpeg.exe. If I reuse it as-is, the user picks a .bin/.gguf and the dialog only shows .exe files (on Windows, the filter hides everything else unless you switch to "所有文件"). So the `kind` parameter is the minimal way to reuse the existing dialog instead of writing a second one.

Alternatives:
1. Leave browseFile untouched and write a dedicated `/api/browse-model` + `browse_model()` function → more code (new endpoint + new backend method) duplicating ~15 lines of tkinter boilerplate.
2. Make browseFile take an optional filter (what I did) → 3 files touched slightly, zero duplication.
3. Pass nothing and let users switch to "所有文件" manually → zero code, but a dialog that appears to "not show my model" is a real usability trap.

Also note: `title` too — otherwise the dialog says "选择文件" and users can't tell whether they're picking the main model or the FA. That's the other param.

Let me write the handler edit carefully.

Current:
```js
// 换模型 → 套用
$("#model-select").addEventListener("change", async e => {
  updateArch();

New:
```js
// 换模型 → 套用；选中「从本地选择…」则先开原生对话框（动作项，不套用）
$("#model-select").addEventListener("change", async e => {
  const opt = e.target.selectedOptions[0];
  if (opt && opt.dataset.action === "browse") { await pickLocalModel("model"); return; }
  updateArch();
  await applyModel(_curCore, e.target.value);
});

// 本地自选模型：选文件 → 写 settings → 重建下拉（kind: "model" 主模型）
async function pickLocalModel(kind) {
  let r = null;
  try { r = await API.browseFile("", T("model.pickLocalTitle", "选择本地模型文件"), kind); }
  catch (err) { modelMsg(String(err), "warn"); }
  if (!r || !r.ok || !r.path) { await refreshModelOptions(); return; }  // 取消 → 回弹
  let res = null;
  try { res = await API.setLocalModel(r.path, kind); }
  catch (err) { modelMsg(T("model.applyFail", "套用失败：") + (err.message || err), "warn"); }
  if (res && res.error) modelMsg(res.error, "warn");
  await refreshModelOptions(res && res.ok ? res.message : null);
}

// 重取目录+当前选择并重建下拉（不重画设备/自检，避免整页闪动）
async function refreshModelOptions(message) {
  try {
    _modelOpt = await API.getModelOptions();
    _curCore = _modelOpt.current.core;
    renderCoreCards();
    populateModels(_curCore, _modelOpt.current.model);
    if (message) modelMsg(message, "info");
    updateLoadBtn();
  } catch (e) {}
}
```

Adding an unused-key fallback is acceptable but sloppy. Better: use existing key "common.browse"? No. I'll add the key to i18n.js now via the CRLF-preserving script later, or just use a plain Chinese string since the backend already returns Chinese labels. The project convention uses T() everywhere. I'll add two keys to i18n.js in branch 2 (when I rename sections) to avoid touching CRLF file twice. Hmm, but leaving T() with a fallback not in dict means English UI shows Chinese. Branch 2 will add them. 刚才那行只做了一件事：给 action 项打标记。

```js
if (m.action) o.dataset.action = m.action;   // "browse" = 动作项（不是真模型）
```

下拉里现在混了两类东西：真模型（预设 10 项 + 已选中的本地档）和一个**动作**（`从本地选择…`）。`<select>` 只能通过 `change` 事件告诉你"选中了第几项"，分不出是哪一类。打了 `dataset.action` 之后，处理函数可以判断：

```js
if (opt.dataset.action === "browse") { /* 开对话框 */ return; }
```

这样就不用在 JS 里硬比较中文字符串 `"从本地选择…"`——那句话只在后端定义一次。

─── [165] 助手 deepseek-flash ───
〔思考〕
The user asks two things:

1. When the local choice is cleared (empty), will the dropdown show "本地模型：无" or just the normal model? — Answer: in `get_model_options`, the dynamic local entry is only appended `if raw_local:` (non-empty), so an empty/cleared value means the entry disappears entirely; the dropdown falls back to preset labels, and `_current_selection` won't return a 本地模型 label. So no "本地模型：无" entry.

But careful: is there any path where the local file exists but the label would be missing? No.

Also `selected_model_present` for empty → falls to preset checks. Good.

2. Why `patch = dict(patch)`? Explain: `patch` comes from the catalog entry — an object shared with the module-level `_MODEL_CATALOG` list. Mutating it would permanently pollute the catalog: `_MODEL_CATALOG` entries' patch dicts are reused by `get_model_options`? No — `get_model_options` only uses labels/backend, not patch. But `selected_model_present_for(backend, patch)` in CLI uses patches from the catalog; if I had injected `crisp_local_model: ""` into the catalog dict, CLI profiles would silently rewrite that key. And more importantly it'd be a global side effect on a module-level constant — a real bug across calls.

So `dict(patch)` = copy before mutation to avoid mutating the shared catalog dict. Alternatively use `{**patch, "crisp_local_model": ""}` without rebinding:

```python
cur = self.settings.update({"backend": backend, **patch,
                            **({"crisp_local_model": ""} if backend == "crispasr" else {})})
```
or
```python
extra = {"crisp_local_model": ""} if backend == "crispasr" else {}
cur = self.settings.update({"backend": backend, **patch, **extra})
```
That avoids the reassignment (which shadows the tuple-unpacked `patch` and can confuse readers). The user seems to prefer the clearer form. Let me switch to the `extra` version — no rebinding, no mutation.

Let me make that edit and answer both questions clearly.

1. **不会出现「本地模型：无」**。动态项是条件追加的：

```python
raw_local = str(s.get("crisp_local_model") or "").strip()
if raw_local:                      # 空 = 压根不追加这一项
    by_core[_ENGINE_CASR].append({"label": _LOCAL_PREFIX + Path(raw_local).name, ...})
```

清空后下拉只剩「预设 10 项 + 从本地选择…」，`_current_selection()` 也不会返回 `本地模型：` 开头的东西，所以预设项会正常被选中。

2. **`dict(patch)` 是防污染，不是多此一举**。`patch` 是 `_MODEL_CATALOG` 里那个字典对象本身：

```python
entry = next(e for e in _MODEL_CATALOG if ...)
core_label, model_label, backend, patch = entry     # patch 指向目录里的 dict
patch["crisp_local_model"] = ""                     # ← 会永久改掉模块级常量
```

+#   crisp_local_model → 主模型（Whisper GGML / Qwen3-ASR GGUF；后端由
+#                       crisp_engine._infer_backend 依文件名推断）
+#   crisp_local_fa    → 时间轴对齐器（ForcedAligner GGUF）。官方文档：
+#                       Qwen3-ForcedAligner 可挂在任何转录后端上，含 Whisper。
+# 下拉里的两个特殊条目：已选中的「本地模型：<文件名>」与动作项「从本地选择…」。
+_LOCAL_PICK = "从本地选择…"
+_LOCAL_PREFIX = "本地模型："
+_LOCAL_MIN_BYTES = 1 << 20      # 1 MB 下限：拦下空档 / Git-LFS pointer
+
+
+def _local_file_ok(p) -> bool:
+    """路径指向的档存在且大小像完整模型（排除空档与 LFS pointer）。"""
+    try:
+        p = Path(p).expanduser()
+        return p.is_file() and p.stat().st_size >= _LOCAL_MIN_BYTES
+    except (OSError, TypeError, ValueError):
+        return False
+
+
+def _local_model(s: dict, key: str = "crisp_local_model") -> Path | None:
+    """settings[key] 指向的本地模型档；未设／失效一律 None（调用端回退预设）。"""
+    raw = str((s or {}).get(key) or "").strip()
+    if not raw:
+        return None
+    p = Path(raw).expanduser()
+    return p if _local_file_ok(p) else None
+

注：没打算测试。
……等一下，CrispASR不是把所有模型一视同仁的吗？？？

附另一AI的回复：
---
这段来自“某AI”的言论，如果属实，那简直是**精准踩中了软件工程里的经典反模式（Anti-pattern）**。必须得狠狠地批判一下，尤其是考虑到你正准备开 PR 做“自定义模型路径”功能！

以下是对这段言论的深度批判与拆解：

### 🚨 批判点一：靠“文件名”猜类型，极其脆弱的“屎山”逻辑
`_infer_backend()` 靠检查文件名里有没有 `qwen3` 来推断后端架构，这种做法在技术上极其荒谬。
*   **文件名是用户资产，不是程序状态**。用户下载模型后，为了管理方便，极大概率会把长长的名字改成 `qwen.gguf`、`model_q8.gguf` 甚至 `测试用.gguf`。
*   **GGUF 标准形同虚设**：GGUF 格式最大的优势之一，就是它的头部（Header）包含了丰富的元数据（Metadata），其中就包括 `general.architecture`（架构名称，如 `qwen2`、`whisper` 等）。放着好好的内部元数据不读，去匹配文件名，这纯粹是为了赶进度写的“偷懒代码”。

### 🛑 批判点二：甩锅给“报错”，掩盖体验的灾难
那个 AI 说：“报错而不是静默跑错，但体验上不友好”。
*   **这根本不是体验好不好的问题，这是 Bug**。用户传入了一个合法的、经过转换的 Qwen GGUF，只因为改了名字，程序就判断它是 Whisper，然后强行用 Whisper 的算子去跑，最后因为维度不匹配崩溃报错。这不叫“有兜底”，这叫**“由于前端的愚蠢，导致后端的无辜崩溃”**。

### 🎯 批判点三：职责错位（前端造轮子，后端背黑锅）
STTMiniTool 作为一个套壳前端，它**绝对不应该**自己去“猜”模型是什么架构。
*   **CrispASR 本身就是通才**。底层引擎已经内置了强大的自动检测能力（通过 GGUF 的元数据），只要你把路径传给它（`-m path/to/model.gguf`），它自己就知道该用哪套推理逻辑。
*   这个 `_infer_backend()` 的存在，说明作者在封装底层 CLI 时，为了在 GUI 上动态显示“当前使用的是 Qwen 还是 Whisper”，搞了个硬编码的字符串匹配。

---

### 🛠️ 对你 PR 的直接启发：怎么绕开这个坑？

既然你打算做“自定义模型路径”，这段“某AI”的情报对你极其宝贵，它直接指明了你要修改的靶点。

**你的 PR 方案可以这样写（让作者无法拒绝）：**

1.  **废除或重构 `_infer_backend()`**：
    不要再读文件名了！如果非要在界面上显示当前用的是什么模型，去读 GGUF 的 Header 信息。Python 下用 `gguf` 这个包，两行代码就能读出 `general.architecture`。
    ```python
    from gguf import GGUFReader
    reader = GGUFReader("model.gguf")
    arch = reader.get_field("general.architecture") # 会返回 'qwen2' 或 'whisper'
    ```
2.  **把“猜测”的权利交还给用户（Fallback）**：
    如果读取元数据失败，在 UI 上加一个下拉框：**“强制指定后端架构”**（选项：自动检测 / Qwen3-ASR / Whisper / Voxtral）。用户选什么，前端就传什么给 CrispASR 的 `--backend` 参数。
3.  **最简单的解法：直接不传 `--backend`**：
    你在 PR 里可以告诉作者：“既然我们是把绝对路径传给底层 `crispasr.exe`，我们完全可以不传 `--backend` 参数，让 CrispASR 自己去读元数据自动识别。这样前端代码最干净，且兼容未来所有新出的模型。”

### 💡 总结
那个 AI 的情报很准，不仅点出了一个隐蔽的 Bug，还为你指明了 PR 的切入点。你可以借着修复这个 Bug 的名义，把“自定义模型路径”的功能做得极其优雅。

在 PR 描述里，你可以直接对作者说：
> “Hi, while implementing the custom model path feature, I noticed `_infer_backend()` relies on filename string matching. This causes issues if users rename their `.gguf` files. I've refactored it to either parse the GGUF header for `general.architecture` or simply let the CrispASR CLI auto-detect it. This makes custom model loading seamless.”

这不是 Vibe 了，这是直接帮他修底层的技术债！去干吧！

─── [208] 返回 run_commands ｜ sed -n '116,128p' crisp_engine.py ───
def _infer_backend(model_path: Path | str) -> str:
    """依模型文件名推断 crispasr 后端（--backend）。

    CrispASR 是多后端 runtime（whisper / qwen3 / parakeet …），同一支
    crispasr.exe 靠 `--backend` 切换模型架构。OpenAI Whisper GGML 属 whisper
    架构（默认，免标志）；Qwen3-ASR GGUF 文件名含 'qwen3' → 走 qwen3 后端，1.7B
    用 'qwen3-1.7b'。判断错误时 crispasr 会自报模型不符，不致默默产生垃圾。
    """
    name = Path(model_path).name.lower()
    if "qwen3" in name:
        return "qwen3-1.7b" if "1.7b" in name else "qwen3"
    return "whisper"

# 功能最全且 -pp 有真实进度行。…仅在挂 FA 时才显式 --backend whisper

对方那段里三条的成色：

| 说法 | 判定 |
|---|---|
| `_infer_backend` 靠文件名猜，很脆弱 | **成立**，尤其"用户自选模型"场景——本地文件最容易被改名 |

| 读 GGUF 的 `general.architecture` 两行搞定 | **半对半错**：GGUF 确实有这个字段，但本项目的 Whisper 是 whisper.cpp 的 **GGML `.bin`**，压根不是 GGUF、没有该字段；且 crispasr 的后端名是 `qwen3-1.7b`（带尺寸），不是纯 arch 串，仍要一层映射；还会新增 `gguf` 依赖 |

--- Content ---
# CrispASR

**One C++ binary, 148 backends — 71 of them TTS engines — plus multilingual text translation, zero Python dependencies.**

CrispASR started as a fork of [whisper.cpp](https://github.com/ggml-org/whisper.cpp) and extends that base into a **unified speech engine** called `crispasr`, backed by full ggml C++ runtimes for major open-weights ASR *and* TTS architectures. One build, one binary, one consistent CLI — pick the backend at the command line or let CrispASR auto-detect it from your GGUF file. See [Text-to-Speech](#text-to-speech-models) for the TTS side.

```console
$ crispasr -m ggml-base.en.bin          -f samples/jfk.wav                    # OpenAI Whisper
$ crispasr -m parakeet-tdt-0.6b.gguf    -f samples/jfk.wav                    # NVIDIA Parakeet
$ crispasr -m canary-1b-v2.gguf         -f samples/jfk.wav                    # NVIDIA Canary
$ crispasr -m voxtral-mini-3b-2507.gguf -f samples/jfk.wav                    # Mistral Voxtral
$ crispasr --backend qwen3 -m auto      -f samples/jfk.wav                    # -m auto downloads
$ crispasr --backend kokoro -m auto --tts "Hello world" --tts-output out.wav  # TTS
```

No Python. No PyTorch. No separate per-model binary. No `pip install`. Just one C++ binary and a GGUF file.

**Browser**: All backends compile to WebAssembly (4.3 MB) via `build-wasm.sh`.
Multit
…（原文 50398 字）

〔思考〕
Key finding from upstream README:

> "One build, one binary, one consistent CLI — **pick the backend at the command line or let CrispASR auto-detect it from your GGUF file**."

And examples show omitting `--backend` for whisper/parakeet/canary/voxtral:
```
$ crispasr -m ggml-base.en.bin          -f samples/jfk.wav   # OpenAI Whisper
$ crispasr -m parakeet-tdt-0.6b.gguf    -f samples/jfk.wav   # NVIDIA Parakeet
```
So auto-detect exists for GGUF. The other AI was right that upstream supports auto-detection.

BUT: the project's实测 (0.8.44–0.8.49) says omitting `--backend` routes to legacy whisper main path (with `-pp` progress but no CTC aligner). That's consistent: whisper model + no flag → whisper legacy path. Auto-detection from GGUF presumably applies to GGUF files (non-whisper). The nuance: whisper's own `.bin` is not GGUF, so "auto-detect from your GGUF file" doesn't cover whisper .bin; it defaults to whisper.

So the truth: upstream DOES auto-detect backend from GGUF metadata. Omitting `--backend` with a Qwen3 GGUF would likely work (auto-detect). But the project's comment says omitting → legacy whisper path... which may be about whisper models only ("whisper 平时不传：省略 --backend 时 crispasr 走 legacy whisper 主路径"). Reading carefully: that sentence is in the context of whisper models. So both can be true:
- whisper model (no flag) → legacy whisper path
- non-whisper GGUF (no flag) → auto-detect

Hmm. The comment says "非 whisper 架构（Qwen3-ASR 等）需显式指定后端，crispasr 才用对解码路径" — that claims explicit is needed for qwen3. That may be outdated relative to upstream (auto-detect was added at some point) or may reflect实测 on the version they used.

Let me verify with upstream docs/cli.md for the `--backend` section and auto-detection details. Fetch raw docs/cli.md and grep. Also check whether qwen3 backend requires the size variant (`qwen3-1.7b`), and whether auto-detect handles Qwen3-ASR GGUF (arch string `qwen3`? or `qwen3-asr`?).

This matters: if auto-detect works for GGUF, then for **local model** selection we could simply not pass `--backend` for non-whisper files — but then whisper `.bin` legacy path vs unified... For local files:
- If file is a whisper GGML .bin → omit `--backend` → whisper legacy (works, but FA ignored; and FA is the fixed preset in plan 1, so FA would be silently ignored!). Wait — plan1 says FA always preset. If local whisper model + FA enabled, `_build_cmd` passes `--backend whisper` (unified_whisper) → OK, since `_infer_backend` returns whisper and use_aligner true → `--backend whisper` is passed. Fine.
- If local file is a Qwen GGUF with arbitrary name → `_infer_backend` returns whisper → passes nothing (word_level + use_aligner → unified_whisper → `--backend whisper`!). That's actively wrong: it would force whisper dispatch on a Qwen3 GGUF. Bad.

So the fix has real value: for local models, don't guess — either omit `--backend` entirely (let upstream auto-detect) or let the user pick.

But careful: omitting `--backend` for a whisper .bin gives legacy path, which ignores `-am/-falign` → FA silently lost. That's the project's known trap. So "always omit" breaks FA for whisper local models.

Therefore the clean design for local models:
- Try auto-detect for GGUF (omit --backend) — but then FA/`-sow`/progress behavior differ by path...
- Better: give the user a small "后端" selector for local files: 自动（按文件）/ Whisper / Qwen3-ASR. Default 自动.

Hmm, but "自动" needs a concrete value to pass or not. Options:
1. 不传 --backend（让上游自动识别）→ whisper .bin 走 legacy（无 FA）
2. --backend whisper（统一 dispatch，FA 生效）
3. --backend qwen3-1.7b

Since prompt says PR1 frozen, I'll report findings and propose this as a follow-up topic (PR2.5 or part of PR2).

Let me fetch docs/cli.md to confirm the `--backend` auto behavior and whether `--backend auto` is a valid value, and how qwen3 sizes are named. That's important for the recommendation.

Let me fetch: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/CrispStrobe/CrispASR/main/docs/cli.md with a prompt for --backend, auto-detect, whisper legacy path, and -am/-falign compatibility.

# CLI reference

`crispasr` extends upstream whisper.cpp's argument set with a handful
of backend-dispatch flags. Every historical whisper flag still works —
when you don't pass `--backend`, the backend is auto-detected from the
model (filename heuristics first, then the GGUF `general.architecture`);
a `ggml-*.bin` resolves to whisper.

## Contents

- [Quick reference](#quick-reference) — most-used flag patterns
- [Core flags](#core) — model, backend, input, language, GPU backend
  - [Model resolution flags](#model-resolution-flags) — `--model-quant`, `--auto-download`, `--cache-dir`, `--hf-repo`
  - [TTS-specific flags](#tts-specific-flags) — voice, instruct, codec, steps, trim
- [Output formats](#output) — txt / srt / vtt / json / csv / lrc
- [Segmentation & chunking](#segmentation--chunking) — VAD, fixed chunks
- [Word-level timestamps via CTC alignment](#word-level-timestamps-via-ctc-alignment) — incl. standalone `--align-only`
- [Sampling / decoding](#sampling--decoding-whisper--llm-backends) — temperature, beam, grammar
- [Language detection (LID)](#language-detection-lid)
- [Diarization](#diarization) — `--diarize`, pyannote, embedder-based clustering
- [LLM-backend specific](#llm-backend-specific) — aligner, max-new-tokens
- [Multi-language / translation](#multi-language--translation)
- [Threading & processors](#threa

## Goal
为 STTMiniTool 的 CrispASR 引擎添加"从本地选择模型文件"功能：方案1（仅主模型，先做）→ 方案2（本地 FA 对齐器，稍后）→ 写 PR 文案，每方案独立分支。

  - 已梳理模型选取链路：`model_catalog._MODEL_CATALOG`（唯一真理源）→ `set_model()` 写 settings → `_current_selection()`/`_identity_for()` 反推 → `_load_crispasr()` 决定路径 → `CrispWhisperEngine._infer_backend()` 仅凭文件名推断后端
  - **方案1 已全部实现并提交**：分支 `feat/crispasr-local-main-model`（从 `main@2ed8110` 切出），提交 `a44b1b8`，5 文件 +191/−12，工作区干净

## Highlights
- **换行符是混的，仓库无 `.gitattributes`，`core.autocrlf` 未设**：
  - LF：`webview_backend.py`、`crisp_engine.py`、`model_catalog.py`、`index.html`、`bridge.js`、`modelView.js`
  - CRLF：`webview_server.py`、`i18n.js`、`downloader.py` — 改这些用 Python 字节级补丁脚本保留 `\r\n`
- **`_local_model()` 语义是"现在还能不能用"**，非"读设置"：`is_file() and size >= 1MB`（拦空档/LFS pointer）。文件消失回 `None` → `_has_any_model` 跳过该项不误报 True
- **刻意分两层**：下拉回显用原始 settings 字符串（文件删了仍显示"本地模型：x"，用户看得见自己的选择没了）；`selected_model_present`/`_has_any_model`/health 明细用 `_local_model()`（缺失即未就绪并给出绝对路径），**不静默回退预设**
- **`set_model` 清 `crisp_local_model` 仅在 `backend == "crispasr"` 时**（切 OpenVINO 不清）；且**不可原地改 `patch`**——它指向 `_MODEL_CATALOG` 里的 dict（模块级常量），会污染 CLI 的 `selected_model_present_for(backend, patch)`。改用 `{**patch, **clear_local}`
- `_identity_for()` 纳入 `crisp_local_model` 路径 → 换档即视为换模型、需重新加载
- `browse_file()` 加 `kind`/`title` 两个**带默认值**参数（默认 `"exe"`，ffmpeg 老路径行为不变）；用户已确认选此方案而非另开端点
- 自检面板中本地模型**单列 `local_model` 一项**，不并入 whisper 项（它可能是 Qwen3 或其它 CrispASR 后端模型）
- **已知限制**：`_infer_backend()` 只认文件名含 `qwen3`；被改名的 Qwen GGUF 会被当 whisper（报错而非静默跑错）
- 过程中两次误删原有代码/注释（`_load_crispasr` 一行注释、`crispasr_dir = self._crispasr_dir()`），均已恢复

〔思考〕
This is the money quote from docs/cli.md:

> "Every historical whisper flag still works — when you don't pass `--backend`, the backend is **auto-detected from the model (filename heuristics first, then the GGUF `general.architecture`)**; a `ggml-*.bin` resolves to whisper."

So upstream auto-detect ALSO uses filename heuristics first (lol — the AI's criticism of filename matching applies to upstream itself!), then GGUF arch. And whisper .bin → whisper.

So: the other AI's claim "don't pass --backend, it auto-detects from metadata" is correct — and importantly it means **the outer tool doesn't need `_infer_backend` at all**. But: the project's comment "省略 --backend 时 crispasr 走 legacy whisper 主路径" refers to whisper .bin resolving to whisper then legacy path — consistent.

However! There's a subtlety: when auto-detect resolves whisper, does the whisper path use legacy or unified? The project实测 said omitting → legacy whisper main path (no aligner). And with `--backend whisper` explicit → unified dispatch (aligner works). So auto-detect whisper = legacy. Hmm, but does auto-detect + `--backend` absent differ from explicit `--backend whisper` for non-whisper? Probably auto-detect just sets the backend the same way, so behavior identical to explicit... except whisper apparently takes a different code path when flag absent. Actually maybe the difference: explicit `--backend whisper` forces the unified dispatch path, while absent → legacy whisper.cpp main. That's the project's实测.

So for the design decision on a local model:
- Whisper .bin local file: omit → legacy (no aligner) ✗ if FA enabled; explicit `--backend whisper` → unified ✓.
- Qwen GGUF renamed: omit → auto-detect by GGUF arch → correct backend ✓ (assuming arch→backend mapping covers qwen3, likely `qwen3` not `qwen3-1.7b`... but auto should handle).
- But if we omit for qwen, we also omit `--lid-model` logic in the project (which is gated on `_backend != "whisper"`). Optional.

So the ideal: don't drop `--backend` globally; instead, for **local model** files, either (a) don't pass `--backend` when the file is GGUF (let upstream sniff arch), or (b) let the user pick backend explicitly. And note upstream itself does filename heuristic first, so "only filename" is upstream's own first pass — but upstream has the GGUF metadata fallback, ours doesn't.

Also: the user's question "CrispASR不是把所有模型一视同仁的吗" — answer: same binary/CLI, auto-detect exists, but whisper .bin is special-cased to whisper and the whisper path has two variants (legacy vs unified). So not 100% "一视同仁", but close.

Now, is the project's claim "非 whisper 架构需显式 --backend" wrong? Possibly it's based on old versions or they just didn't try. Upstream docs say auto-detect works. But careful: maybe crispasr 0.8.44 didn't have it, or Qwen3's arch string wasn't recognized. The project comment says it as fact. Worth noting: it might be outdated/over-cautious — testable by one command with a renamed qwen gguf.

Now for answer: give the verdict table, the decisive quote, and what it means for PR. Then propose the concrete option. Since PR1 is frozen and user doesn't want to test, recommend: keep PR1 as-is, and treat this as a follow-up improvement in the local-model feature (a "后端" selector with default 自动 = don't pass --backend) — that's actually cleaner and matches upstream design, and we can drop `_infer_backend` dependence for local files entirely.

Key content:
1. 结论：CrispASR 是"一个二进制、多后端"，但**不是**完全一视同仁：省略 --backend 时自动识别，识别顺序 = 先文件名启发式，再 GGUF 的 general.architecture；ggml-*.bin 一律 = whisper。引用 docs/cli.md 首段。
2. 所以你上一轮的实测记录和上游文档不矛盾：whisper .bin 自动识别结果就是 whisper，而 whisper 有 legacy / 统一 dispatch 两条路，省略标志走 legacy → FA 被忽略。这解释了 version.py 里的记录。

Let me write.

拿到决定性证据了，`docs/cli.md` 开头第一段：

> Every historical whisper flag still works — when you don't pass `--backend`, the backend is **auto-detected from the model (filename heuristics first, then the GGUF `general.architecture`)**; a `ggml-*.bin` resolves to whisper.

所以你的直觉和上轮实测记录都对，只是两件事被混成了一件：

1. **确实有自动识别** —— 对方这条说对了。我之前基于 `version.py` 的实测推断"非 whisper 必须显式传"，是偏保守了。
2. **但自动识别的第一道还是文件名启发式**，GGUF 元数据只是 fallback。所谓"文件名猜测是屎山"，其实是上游自己的第一道兜底，不是本项目的独创。
3. **`ggml-*.bin` 一律解析成 whisper**，而 whisper 恰好有 legacy 主路径和统一 dispatch 两条路——省略标志走 legacy，`-am/-falign` 被静默忽略。这就解释了 `version.py:97` 那条实测，两者不矛盾。

**真正有价值的那条是这个**：本地档改名成 `model.gguf` 的 Qwen3，`_infer_backend` 返回 `whisper`；又因为 FA 常开，`unified_whisper` 为真，于是**显式**发出 `--backend whisper`,把 Qwen 权重硬塞进 whisper 解码路径。这不是"报错也不算静默跑错"，是真会跑错。

最省事的方向反而不是"加 gguf 依赖读 header"，而是**本地档干脆不传 `--backend`，交给上游自动识别**——但 whisper 本地档会因此掉回 legacy、丢掉 FA。那么：这套"本地档免猜后端"你想怎么处理？

如果哪天要给作者看，三句话就够，不用搬对方那篇：

- 上游 `docs/cli.md`：省略 `--backend` 时自动识别，顺序是文件名启发式 → GGUF `general.architecture`；`ggml-*.bin` 解析为 whisper。
- 因此 `_infer_backend()` 的文件名判断在"用户重命名过的 Qwen3 GGUF"上会落到 `whisper`，叠加常开的 FA 还会**显式**传 `--backend whisper`，把权重塞进错的解码路径（不只是报错，是会跑偏）。
- 但也不能一律不传：whisper 本地档会掉回 legacy 主路径，`-am/-falign` 被静默忽略。

这条就当一个已知限制留在原地吧。熊猫把竹子收回筐里了。
